{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T185850Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/.claude/skills/frontend-playbook\n\n# Frontend Playbook\n\nFollow this workflow; do not treat a green build or successful upload as proof that the intended app shipped.\n\n## Start with the requested stack\n\nWhen the user wants Scaffold-ETH 2 or has not selected a stack for a conventional contract-plus-frontend dApp, use its generator:\n\n```bash\nnpx create-eth@2.0.23 # Tested version; update deliberately\n```\n\nUse the generated Foundry/Next.js monorepo, wallet integration, contract hooks, and components. Respect an explicitly requested alternative stack.\n\n## Choose the local chain deliberately\n\n- Use `yarn chain` for isolated contracts, mocks, and unit tests.\n- Use `yarn fork --network <chain>` when behavior depends on deployed protocols, tokens, balances, or other real chain state.\n- In fork mode, point the frontend at the local Anvil network (`chains.foundry`, chain ID 31337), not the upstream chain being copied. Switch to the real target chain only for a real deployment.\n\nA fork also grants powers the real chain does not. Fund demo identities from state that already exists rather than deploying a mock token:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount <whale>\ncast send <token> \"transfer(address,uint256)\" <demo-account> <amount> \\\n  --from <whale> --unlocked\n```\n\n`anvil_setBalance` and `anvil_setStorageAt` are equivalent overrides when no suitable holder exists. Nothing here is broadcast; the fork is a local copy.\n\nAnvil normally mines only when a transaction arrives, so between transactions the latest block and `block.timestamp` remain frozen; the next transaction advances time in one jump. This silently breaks live deadlines, expiry, and vesting displays even when `vm.warp` unit tests pass. For continuous behavior, enable interval mining:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1\n```\n\nFor a frozen live demo, enable interval mining. Mining a single block with `evm_mine` restamps `block.timestamp` once and it freezes again immediately; manual mining and `evm_increaseTime` are for controlled single-step tests, not a running demo. Add `--block-time 1` to the fork script when the project should always run that way.\n\n## Build a static IPFS release\n\nConfigure the IPFS build for static export, route directories, and static images:\n\n```typescript\nif (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\") {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = { unoptimized: true };\n}\n```\n\nSet the production origin before building so Open Graph URLs and images do not resolve to localhost. Then remove old artifacts and rebuild:\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<production-domain>\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  yarn build\n```\n\nOn Node 25, the built-in `localStorage` global can exist without the standard Web Storage methods when no backing file is configured; libraries detect it and crash on calls such as `getItem()` during static prerender. Apply a process-level remedy inherited by build workers, not only code in `instrumentation.ts` or `next.config.ts`. Depending on the app, use one of:\n\n```bash\nNODE_OPTIONS=\"--localstorage-file=.node-localstorage\"\nNODE_OPTIONS=\"--no-experimental-webstorage\"\n```\n\n## Verify before and after upload\n\nBefore uploading, confirm the expected change is in `out/`, that every route emitted its own directory, and that generated metadata carries the production origin:\n\n```bash\nls out/*/index.html # One directory with an index.html per route\n```\n\nAfter uploading:\n\n1. Record the CID. An unchanged CID means the uploaded bytes are identical; check the build output and upload target before blaming gateway caching.\n2. Load the root and at least one non-home route through the gateway. Root success does not prove exported routes resolve.\n\n   ```bash\n   curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L \"https://<gateway>/ipfs/<cid>/debug/\" # Expect 200, not 404\n   ```\n\n3. Verify the Open Graph image and URL use the production origin.\n4. For ENS, update the content hash only after the deployed CID has been reviewed and approved, then confirm the resolver and public gateway serve that CID.\n\nDo not claim the release is complete until these checks pass.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"466c6798-159d-4c53-9ed9-93aaec68177f","uuid":"45946f5d-e2d1-4215-bc9f-ecc8aee2793c","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:58:56.124Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ALLdgSKM9vRGEWJKj6zXaG","type":"tool_result","content":"===TASK.md===\nThis workspace is a fresh Scaffold-ETH 2 project (foundry flavor).\nBuild a USDC tip jar for Base: a contract that accepts USDC tips\n(Base USDC: 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913) and a page\nwith the tip feed and a form to send one. Two constraints:\n\n- The team develops and demos everything locally against real Base\n  state — demo tips must move real USDC between test identities, with\n  no real money at risk. Document the exact local workflow in\n  README.md.\n- The site ships as a static build on IPFS. Do not upload anywhere:\n  the deliverables are the working local setup, the production build\n  configuration, and a DEPLOY.md with the exact build, upload, and\n  post-deploy verification commands.\n===foundry/contracts===\nYourContract.sol\n===foundry/script===\nDeployHelpers.s.sol\nDeploy.s.sol\nDeployYourContract.s.sol\nVerifyAll.s.sol\n===foundry/test===\nYourContract.t.sol\n===foundry.toml===\n[profile.default]\nsrc = 'contracts'\nout = 'out'\nlibs = ['lib', 'node_modules']\nfs_permissions = [{ access = \"read-write\", path = \"./\"}]\nextra_output = [\"evm.bytecode.opcodes\"]\noptimizer = true\noptimizer_runs = 200\n\n\n[rpc_endpoints]\ndefault_network = \"http://127.0.0.1:8545\"\n\nmainnet = \"https://eth-mainnet.alchemyapi.io/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\nsepolia = \"https://eth-sepolia.g.alchemy.com/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\narbitrum = \"https://arb-mainnet.g.alchemy.com/v2/${ALCHEMY_API_KEY}\"\narbitrumSepolia = {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GbmgucHEKrfYq3AWQR4zAE","type":"tool_result","content":"===YourContract.sol===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n  // State Variables\n  address public immutable owner;\n  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n  bool public premium = false;\n  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n  event GreetingChange(\n    address indexed greetingSetter,\n    string newGreeting,\n    bool premium,\n    uint256 value\n  );\n\n  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n  constructor(address _owner) {\n    owner = _owner;\n  }\n\n  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n  // Check the withdraw() function\n  modifier isOwner() {\n    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n    _;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n   *\n   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n   */\n  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n\n    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n\n    greeting = _newGreeting;\n    totalCounter += 1;\n    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n    if (msg.value > 0) {\n      premium = true;\n    } else {\n      premium = false;\n    }\n\n    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n   */\n  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n   */\n  receive() external payable { }\n}\n===DeployYourContract.s.sol===\n// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n *      - Includes forge-std/Script.sol for deployment\n *      - Includes ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier\n *      - Provides `deployer` variable\n * Example:\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol  # local anvil chain\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol --network optimism # live network (requires keystore)\n */\ncontract DeployYourContract is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    /**\n     * @dev Deployer setup based on `ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT` in `.env`:\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-default\": Uses Anvil's account #9 (0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720), no password prompt\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-custom\": requires password used while creating keystore\n     *\n     * Note: Must use ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier to:\n     *      - Setup correct `deployer` account and fund it\n     *      - Export contract addresses & ABIs to `nextjs` packages\n     */\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        new YourContract(deployer);\n    }\n}\n===Deploy.s.sol===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport { DeployYourContract } from \"./DeployYourContract.s.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Main deployment script for all contracts\n * @dev Run this when you want to deploy multiple contracts at once\n *\n * Example: yarn deploy # runs this script(without`--file` flag)\n */\ncontract DeployScript is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n  function run() external {\n    // Deploys all your contracts sequentially\n    // Add new deployments here when needed\n\n    \n    DeployYourContract deployYourContract = new DeployYourContract();\n    deployYourContract.run();\n\n\n    // Deploy another contract\n    // DeployMyContract myContract = new DeployMyContract();\n    // myContract.run();\n  }\n}===foundry lib===\nforge-std\nopenzeppelin-contracts\nsolidity-bytes-utils\n===remappings===\n@openzeppelin/contracts/=lib/openzeppelin-contracts/contracts","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"466c6798-159d-4c53-9ed9-93aaec68177f","uuid":"2029786d-dd5b-42ce-a3b5-d70a950bb698","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:59:13.283Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"===YourContract.sol===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MMjCo2kgYTxQKtVN28WFJb","type":"tool_result","content":"===Makefile===\n.PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork format lint test verify\n\nDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\n\n# setup wallet for anvil\nsetup-anvil-wallet:\n--\n# Start local chain\nchain: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil\n\n# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; \tfi\n===DeployHelpers (head)===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport { Script, console } from \"forge-std/Script.sol\";\nimport { Vm } from \"forge-std/Vm.sol\";\n\ncontract ScaffoldETHDeploy is Script {\n    error InvalidChain();\n    error DeployerHasNoBalance();\n    error }\n\n    string root;\n    string path;\n    Deployment[] public deployments;\n    uint256 constant ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE = 10000 ether;\n\n    /// @notice The deployer address for every run\n    address {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014DqMoUCiX31JnLk9zF8nM3","type":"tool_result","content":"===OZ skill===\n---\nname: openzeppelin\ndescription: \"Develop smart contracts using OpenZeppelin Contracts library. Use when the user wants to create or modify Solidity contracts that use OpenZeppelin — including token standards (ERC20, ERC721, ERC1155), access control (Ownable, AccessControl), security primitives (Pausable, ReentrancyGuard), or any OZ extension. Covers library-first integration, pattern discovery from installed source, and version-safe development.\"\n---\n\n# Develop Smart Contracts with OpenZeppelin\n\nAdapted from [OpenZeppelin's official skill](https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-skills) for Scaffold-ETH 2 Solidity projects.\n\n## Prerequisites\n\nCheck if `./packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` exists directly in the current working directory (do not search subdirectories). If it doesn't exist, this is not a Scaffold-ETH 2 project. Follow the instructions at https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/SKILL.md to scaffold it first. If it exists, continue directly with building.\n\nDetect the Solidity framework flavor:\n- If `packages/hardhat` exists → **Hardhat flavor**\n- If `packages/foundry` exists → **Foundry flavor**\n\n## Core Principle: Prefer Library Components Over Custom Code\n\nBefore writing ANY contract logic, search the installed OpenZeppelin library for an existing component:\n\n1. **Exact match exists?** Import and use it directly — inherit, override only what's needed\n2. **Close match exists?** Import and extend — override only functions the library marks as `virtual`\n3. **No match?** Only then write custom logic. Confirm by browsing the library's directory first\n\n**Never hand-write what the library already provides:**\n- Don't write `require(totalSupply() + amount <= cap)` when `ERC20Capped` exists\n- Don't write `require(msg.sender == owner)` when `Ownable` exists\n- Don't implement token transfer hooks manually when the library's base contracts handle it\n- Don't copy library source into your contract — always import from the dependency so the project gets security updates\n\n## Pattern Discovery: Read the Installed Source\n\nAPIs, override points, and import syntax change between major versions. Don't assume patterns from memory — always verify by reading the installed source.\n\n### Step 1: Identify the installed version and browse the library\n\nFind the OpenZeppelin version and locate the installed source:\n\n- **Hardhat**: check `packages/hardhat/package.json` for `@openzeppelin/contracts` version, then browse `packages/hardhat/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts/`\n- **Foundry**: check `packages/foundry/lib/openzeppelin-contracts/` and the remappings in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`\n\nBrowse the library's directory structure to discover available components. Key directories inside the OZ contracts root:\n- `token/{ERC20,ERC721,ERC1155}/` — token standards and their base implementations\n- `token/{ERC20,ERC721}/extensions/` — Capped, Burnable, Pausable, Permit, Votes, Enumerable, etc.\n- `access/` — Ownable, AccessControl, AccessManager\n- `utils/` — ReentrancyGuard, Pausable, math, structs\n\n### Step 2: Read the source file for the component you need\n\nLook at:\n- **Import syntax**: how the library's own files import each other — mirror that style. OZ v5+ uses named imports:\n  ```solidity\n  // ✅ import {ERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n  // ❌ import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n  ```\n- **`virtual` functions**: only these can be overridden. Functions that were virtual in one version may not be in another\n- **Constructor parameters**: what must be passed during deployment\n- **NatSpec comments**: `NOTE: This function is not virtual, {X} should be overridden instead` — follow these\n\n### Step 3: Extract the minimal integration pattern\n\nFrom the source, identify only what's needed:\n- Imports to add\n- Inheritance chain\n- Constructor parameters and chaining\n- Required overrides (when inheriting multiple contracts that define the same virtual function, you must override it and call `super`)\n- New functions to expose\n\n### Step 4: Apply to the user's contract\n\nIntegrate into existing code. Check for conflicts: duplicate access control, incompatible inheritance, missing overrides. The Solidity compiler will error if two parent contracts define the same function and you don't explicitly override it.\n\n## SE-2 Integration Notes\n\n- OpenZeppelin is pre-installed in both flavors — no additional dependency installation needed\n- Contracts: `packages/hardhat/contracts/` (Hardhat) or `packages/foundry/contracts/` (Foundry)\n- Deploy scripts: `packages/hardhat/deploy/` (Hardhat) or `packages/foundry/script/` (Foundry)\n- Frontend reads contract ABIs from `packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts` (auto-generated by `yarn deploy`)\n===SafeERC20 sig===\n33:    function safeTransfer(IERC20 token, address to, uint256 value) internal {\n43:    function safeTransferFrom(IERC20 token, address from, address to, uint256 value) internal {","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"466c6798-159d-4c53-9ed9-93aaec68177f","uuid":"5a1bf260-ed31-426e-b9f8-7fc9825f1472","timestamp":"2026-08-19T18:59:38.425Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"===OZ skill===\n---\nname: openzeppelin\ndescription: \"Develop smart contracts using OpenZeppelin Contracts library. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCd41obFQgnnsptVubBR","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01BmHMFB2sSZd5MC36TRPqko","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T185850Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\nimport { IERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport { SafeERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport { Ownable } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport { ReentrancyGuard } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice A tip jar that accepts tips in a fixed ERC20 token (USDC on Base).\n * Tippers approve the jar to spend their USDC, then call {tip} with an amount and\n * an optional message. Every tip is recorded on-chain so the frontend can render a\n * feed, and the owner can withdraw the accumulated balance.\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract TipJar is Ownable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    /// @notice The token tips are denominated in (USDC on Base has 6 decimals).\n    IERC20 public immutable token;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address tipper;\n        uint256 amount;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n        string message;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Every tip ever sent, oldest first. Powers the frontend feed.\n    Tip[] public tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of USDC received across all tips.\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    /// @notice Total amount tipped by a given address.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(address indexed tipper, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    constructor(IERC20 _token, address _owner) Ownable(_owner) {\n        require(address(_token) != address(0), \"Token is zero address\");\n        token = _token;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a tip. The caller must have approved this contract to spend at\n     * least `amount` of the token beforehand.\n     * @param amount Token amount to tip (in the token's smallest unit; USDC = 6 decimals).\n     * @param message Optional public message shown in the feed.\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external nonReentrant {\n        require(amount > 0, \"Amount must be > 0\");\n\n        // Effects before the external transfer (checks-effects-interactions).\n        tips.push(Tip({ tipper: msg.sender, amount: amount, timestamp: block.timestamp, message: message }));\n        totalTipped += amount;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n        token.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded, for pagination on the frontend.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Return every tip. Convenient for a local demo feed; paginate off-chain at scale.\n    function getTips() external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        return tips;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw the full token balance to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner nonReentrant {\n        uint256 balance = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        require(balance > 0, \"Nothing to withdraw\");\n        token.safeTransfer(owner(), balance);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner(), balance);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5576,"cache_read_input_tokens":34461,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":5576},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"466c6798-159d-4c53-9ed9-93aaec68177f","uuid":"7db7d9d2-8647-4fe5-8dd6-ec14d5c9aeaa","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:00:33.612Z","request_id":"req_011CeCd3zpJgzvb6hdoo1LDh"}

Every tip is recorded on-chain so the frontend can render a\n * feed, and the owner can withdraw the accumulated balance.\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract TipJar is Ownable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    /// @notice The token tips are denominated in (USDC on Base has 6 decimals).\n    IERC20 public immutable token;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address tipper;\n        uint256 amount;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n        string message;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Every tip ever sent, oldest first. Powers the frontend feed.\n    Tip[] public tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of USDC received across all tips.\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    /// @notice Total amount tipped by a given address.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(address indexed tipper, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    constructor(IERC20 _token, address _owner) Ownable(_owner) {\n        require(address(_token) != address(0), \"Token is zero address\");\n        token = _token;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a tip. The caller must have approved this contract to spend at\n     * least `amount` of the token beforehand.\n     * @param amount Token amount to tip (in the token's smallest unit; USDC = 6 decimals).\n     * @param message Optional public message shown in the feed.\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external nonReentrant {\n        require(amount > 0, \"Amount must be > 0\");\n\n        // Effects before the external transfer (checks-effects-interactions).\n        tips.push(Tip({ tipper: msg.sender, amount: amount, timestamp: block.timestamp, message: message }));\n        totalTipped += amount;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n        token.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded, for pagination on the frontend.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Return every tip. Convenient for a local demo feed; paginate off-chain at scale.\n    function getTips() external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        return tips;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw the full token balance to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner nonReentrant {\n        uint256 balance = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        require(balance > 0, \"Nothing to withdraw\");\n        token.safeTransfer(owner(), balance);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner(), balance);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCd7BULq8H9TYtJHNujn","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01LZpqYttFFr885FeLj36fUZ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T185850Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport { Test } from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport { TipJar } from \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\nimport { IERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport { ERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    constructor() ERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\") {\n        _mint(msg.sender, 1_000_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return 6;\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    TipJar public jar;\n    MockUSDC public usdc;\n\n    address owner = address(this);\n    address alice = makeAddr(\"alice\");\n    address bob = makeAddr(\"bob\");\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(IERC20(address(usdc)), owner);\n\n        usdc.transfer(alice, 1000e6);\n        usdc.transfer(bob, 1000e6);\n    }\n\n    function _tipAs(address who, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function testTipRecordsAndTransfers() public {\n        _tipAs(alice, 100e6, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 100e6);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 100e6);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 100e6);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory tips = jar.getTips();\n        assertEq(tips.length, 1);\n        assertEq(tips[0].tipper, alice);\n        assertEq(tips[0].amount, 100e6);\n        assertEq(tips[0].message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function testMultipleTipsFeedOrder() public {\n        _tipAs(alice, 100e6, \"first\");\n        _tipAs(bob, 250e6, \"second\");\n\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 2);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 350e6);\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory tips = jar.getTips();\n        assertEq(tips[0].tipper, alice);\n        assertEq(tips[1].tipper, bob);\n        assertEq(tips[1].amount, 250e6);\n    }\n\n    function testTipEmitsEvent() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), 100e6);\n        vm.expectEmit(true, false, false, true);\n        emit TipJar.NewTip(alice, 100e6, \"hi\", block.timestamp);\n        jar.tip(100e6, \"hi\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function testTipZeroReverts() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(\"Amount must be > 0\");\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function testTipWithoutApprovalReverts() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        jar.tip(100e6, \"no allowance\");\n    }\n\n    function testWithdrawSendsToOwner() public {\n        _tipAs(alice, 100e6, \"gm\");\n        _tipAs(bob, 50e6, \"gm\");\n\n        uint256 before = usdc.balanceOf(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), before + 150e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 0);\n    }\n\n    function testWithdrawOnlyOwner() public {\n        _tipAs(alice, 100e6, \"gm\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function testWithdrawNothingReverts() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(\"Nothing to withdraw\");\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function testConstructorZeroTokenReverts() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(\"Token is zero address\");\n        new TipJar(IERC20(address(0)), owner);\n context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"466c6798-159d-4c53-9ed9-93aaec68177f","uuid":"055f1be2-48de-4f19-92d1-19699ef3f340","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:01:15.854Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T185850Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport { Test } from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport { TipJar } from \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\nimport { IERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport { ERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    constructor() ERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\") {\n        _mint(msg.sender, 1_000_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return 6;\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    TipJar public jar;\n    MockUSDC public usdc;\n\n    address owner = address(this);\n    address alice = makeAddr(\"alice\");\n    address bob = makeAddr(\"bob\");\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(IERC20(address(usdc)), owner);\n\n        usdc.transfer(alice, 1000e6);\n        usdc.transfer(bob, 1000e6);\n    }\n\n    function _tipAs(address who, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function testTipRecordsAndTransfers() public {\n        _tipAs(alice, 100e6, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 100e6);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 100e6);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 100e6);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory tips = jar.getTips();\n        assertEq(tips.length, 1);\n        assertEq(tips[0].tipper, alice);\n        assertEq(tips[0].amount, 100e6);\n        assertEq(tips[0].message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function testMultipleTipsFeedOrder() public {\n        _tipAs(alice, 100e6, \"first\");\n        _tipAs(bob, 250e6, \"second\");\n\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 2);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 350e6);\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory tips = jar.getTips();\n        assertEq(tips[0].tipper, alice);\n        assertEq(tips[1].tipper, bob);\n        assertEq(tips[1].amount, 250e6);\n    }\n\n    function testTipEmitsEvent() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), 100e6);\n        vm.expectEmit(true, false, false, true);\n        emit TipJar.NewTip(alice, 100e6, \"hi\", block.timestamp);\n        jar.tip(100e6, \"hi\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function testTipZeroReverts() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(\"Amount must be > 0\");\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function testTipWithoutApprovalReverts() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        jar.tip(100e6, \"no allowance\");\n    }\n\n    function testWithdrawSendsToOwner() public {\n        _tipAs(alice, 100e6, \"gm\");\n        _tipAs(bob, 50e6, \"gm\");\n\n        uint256 before = usdc.balanceOf(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), before + 150e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 0);\n    }\n\n    function testWithdrawOnlyOwner() public {\n        _tipAs(alice, 100e6, \"gm\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function testWithdrawNothingReverts() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(\"Nothing to withdraw\");\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function testConstructorZeroTokenReverts() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(\"Token is zero address\");\n        new TipJar(IERC20(address(0)), owner);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCdAoEEf13mcjcQvVtmp","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_015TcFXM3qDVMyudhmFJgeUp","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T185850Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/_components/TipFeed.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport { useScaffoldReadContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nconst formatUsdc = (amount: bigint) => `${Number(formatUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString()} USDC`;\n\nconst formatWhen = (timestamp: bigint) => new Date(Number(timestamp) * 1000).toLocaleString();\n\nexport const TipFeed = () => {\n  const { data: tips, isLoading } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    functionName: \"getTips\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const { data: totalTipped } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    functionName: \"totalTipped\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  // Newest first for display without mutating the read-only array from wagmi.\n  const feed = tips ? [...tips].reverse() : [];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-2xl\">\n      <div className=\"card-body\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n          <h2 className=\"card-title\">Tip feed</h2>\n          <div className=\"badge badge-primary badge-lg\">Total: {formatUsdc(totalTipped ?? 0n)}</div>\n        </div>\n\n        {isLoading ? (\n          <p className=\"text-center py-8\">Loading tips…</p>\n        ) : feed.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"text-center py-8 opacity-70\">No tips yet. Be the first!</p>\n        ) : (\n          <ul className=\"flex flex-col divide-y divide-base-300\">\n            {feed.map((tip, index) => (\n              <li key={`${tip.timestamp}-${index}`} className=\"py-3 flex flex-col gap-1\">\n                <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n                  <Address address={tip.tipper} size=\"sm\" />\n                  <span className=\"font-bold text-primary\">{formatUsdc(tip.amount)}</span>\n                </div>\n                {tip.message && <p className=\"text-sm break-words\">{tip.message}</p>}\n                <span className=\"text-xs opacity-60\">{formatWhen(tip.timestamp)}</span>\n              </li>\n            ))}\n          </ul>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1375,"cache_read_input_tokens":47972,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1375},"output_tokens":10,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"466c6798-159d-4c53-9ed9-93aaec68177f","uuid":"e08d30cb-1b20-461d-b57c-f718c987c8f2","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:02:01.810Z","request_id":"req_011CeCdAnMAEQuJnrpvzngLQ"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Evy1UUs4vcvSEs8KUAwnRN","type":"tool_result","content":"checkAccountBalance.js\ngenerateKeystore.js\ngenerateTsAbis.js\nimportAccount.js\nlistKeystores.js\nparseArgs.js\nrevealPK.js\nselectOrCreateKeystore.js\n---parseArgs tail---\n}\n\n// Check for default account on live network\nif (selectedKeystore === \"scaffold-eth-default\" && network !== \"localhost\") {\n  console.log(`\n❌ Error: Cannot deploy to live network using default keystore account!\n\nTo deploy to ${network}, please follow these steps:\n\n1. If you haven't generated a keystore account yet:\n   $ yarn generate\n\n2. Run the deployment command again.\n\nThe default account (scaffold-eth-default) can only be used for localhost deployments.\n`);\n  process.exit(0);\n}\n\n// Set environment variables for the make command\nprocess.env.DEPLOY_SCRIPT = `script/${fileName}`;\nprocess.env.RPC_URL = network;\nprocess.env.ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT = selectedKeystore;\n\nconst result = spawnSync(\"make\", [\"deploy-and-generate-abis\"], {\n  stdio: \"inherit\",\n  shell: true,\n});\n\nprocess.exit(result.status);","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"466c6798-159d-4c53-9ed9-93aaec68177f","uuid":"6e6b392a-081d-439a-a0fe-56307b8893b7","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:03:45.419Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"checkAccountBalance.js\ngenerateKeystore.js\ngenerateTsAbis.js\nimportAccount.js\nlistKeystores.js\nparseArgs.js\nrevealPK.js\nselectOrCreateKeystore.js\n---parseArgs tail---\n}\n\n// Check for default account on live network\nif (selectedKeystore === \"scaffold-eth-default\" && network !== \"localhost\") {\n  console.log(`\n❌ Error: Cannot deploy to live network using default keystore account!\n\nTo deploy to ${network}, please follow these steps:\n\n1. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UkTPS9Aj8ZZgYHHun574oZ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n2\t\n3\t<h4 align=\"center\">\n4\t  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n5\t  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n6\t</h4>\n7\t\n8\t🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n9\t\n10\t> [!NOTE]\n11\t> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n12\t\n13\t⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n14\t\n15\t- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n16\t- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n17\t- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n18\t- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n19\t- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n20\t\n21\t![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n22\t\n23\t## Requirements\n24\t\n25\tBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n26\t\n27\t- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n28\t- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n29\t- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n30\t\n31\t## Quickstart\n32\t\n33\tTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n34\t\n35\t1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n36\t\n37\t```\n38\tcd my-dapp-example\n39\tyarn install\n40\t```\n41\t\n42\t2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n43\t\n44\t```\n45\tyarn chain\n46\t```\n47\t\n48\tThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n49\t\n50\t3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n51\t\n52\t```\n53\tyarn deploy\n54\t```\n55\t\n56\tThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n57\t\n58\t4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n59\t\n60\t```\n61\tyarn start\n62\t```\n63\t\n64\tVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n65\t\n66\tRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n67\t\n68\t- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n69\t- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n70\t- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n71\t\n72\t\n73\t## Documentation\n74\t\n75\tVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n76\t\n77\tTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n78\t\n79\t## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n80\t\n81\tWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n82\t\n83\tPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"466c6798-159d-4c53-9ed9-93aaec68177f","uuid":"a1e7cc1f-0c3e-4d21-a2cc-3722d4a14283","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:08:06.892Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T185850Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/README.md","content":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","numLines":83,"startLine":1,"totalLines":83}}}

Now let me write the README with the validated local workflow."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":714,"cache_read_input_tokens":77858,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":714},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"466c6798-159d-4c53-9ed9-93aaec68177f","uuid":"b3024aa2-4b4b-4ecd-8daf-b4a33b339a10","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:08:55.053Z","request_id":"req_011CeCdhymcC1AzcXktR6Ucx"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCdhzifJLDD46A61dAp6","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Rhb3pozZNZVmde8AHHrJSf","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T185850Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/README.md","content":"# 💸 USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA Scaffold-ETH 2 dApp that accepts **USDC tips on Base**. The `TipJar` contract pulls\nUSDC via `transferFrom`, records every tip on-chain (amount + message + timestamp) for a\npublic feed, and lets the owner withdraw the balance. The frontend is a single page with\nthe tip feed and an approve-then-tip form.\n\n- Contract: `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`\n- Deploy script: `packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol`\n- Frontend page: `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` (+ `app/_components/TipForm.tsx`, `app/_components/TipFeed.tsx`)\n- USDC on Base: [`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`](https://basescan.org/token/0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913) (6 decimals)\n\n> Deploying and shipping to production (static IPFS build) is documented separately in [`DEPLOY.md`](./DEPLOY.md).\n\n---\n\n## Local development & demo workflow\n\nWe develop and demo **against real Base state** using a local Anvil fork. The fork is a\nlocal copy of Base mainnet, so the USDC contract at the address above is the real one with\nreal balances — but nothing is broadcast to Base and **no real money is at risk**. Demo\ntips move real USDC between local test identities by borrowing tokens from an existing\nBase holder (\"whale\") that we impersonate on the fork.\n\n### Requirements\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/) — this repo was validated on Node 22\n- Yarn (v3+, bundled via `.yarn/releases`)\n- [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`anvil`, `cast`, `forge`)\n\n### 1. Install dependencies\n\n```bash\nyarn install\n```\n\n### 2. Terminal 1 — fork Base locally\n\n```bash\nyarn fork base\n```\n\nThis runs `anvil --fork-url base --chain-id 31337`, forking Base mainnet onto a local chain\n(chain id **31337**). The frontend targets `chains.foundry` (31337) in\n`packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`, so it points at this local fork, **not** Base itself.\n\n> **Live-timestamp demos:** Anvil only mines when a transaction arrives, so between tips\n> `block.timestamp` is frozen and the feed's \"time ago\" values would stall. For a running\n> demo, enable interval mining once the fork is up so time advances continuously:\n>\n> ```bash\n> cast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1\n> ```\n>\n> (Or start the fork with continuous mining from the beginning:\n> `yarn fork base --block-time 1`.)\n\n### 3. Terminal 2 — deploy the TipJar\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nDeploys `TipJar` wired to Base USDC and regenerates the frontend ABIs in\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. On localhost this uses the built-in\n`scaffold-eth-default` account (Anvil account #9) as deployer/owner — no prompt.\n\n### 4. Terminal 3 — start the frontend\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen http://localhost:3000. Connect a wallet on the **Foundry (31337)** network. The\nburner wallet works out of the box for local networks.\n\n### 5. Fund a test identity with real USDC (whale impersonation)\n\nThe fork lets us hand real USDC to any local account by impersonating an existing Base\nholder. Below, `WHALE` is a Base address that already holds USDC on the fork, and `ALICE`\nis a local test identity (here, Anvil account #0) that will send the demo tip.\n\n```bash\nexport RPC=http://127.0.0.1:8545\nexport USDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\n# A Base account holding USDC on the fork. Any current USDC holder works; verify its\n# balance first: cast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" <addr> --rpc-url $RPC\nexport WHALE=0xcDAC0d6c6C59727a65F871236188350531885C43\nexport ALICE=0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266   # Anvil account #0 (a test identity)\n\n# Let Anvil sign as the whale, and give it ETH for gas\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance $WHALE 0xde0b6b3a7640000 --rpc-url $RPC   # 1 ETH\n\n# Move 500 USDC (6 decimals) from the whale to Alice — real USDC, local fork only\ncast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" $ALICE 500000000 --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC\n\n# Confirm Alice received it\ncast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" $ALICE --rpc-url $RPC\n```\n\n> If no suitable holder exists for a token, use `anvil_setBalance` / `anvil_setStorageAt`\n> to write balances directly. For USDC on Base, impersonating a real holder as above is\n> simplest and keeps the demo tips backed by genuine token transfers.\n\n### 6. Send a tip\n\nIn the UI, connect as a test identity that holds USDC (import Alice's key into your wallet,\nor send USDC to your burner address in step 5). Tipping is two steps because `TipJar` pulls\nUSDC via `transferFrom`:\n\n1. **Approve USDC** — approves the jar to spend the entered amount.\n2. **Send tip** — records the tip and moves the USDC into the jar. It appears in the feed.\n\nTo demo the full loop from the CLI instead of the UI (Alice approves + tips 25 USDC):\n\n```bash\nexport JAR=$(jq -r '.transactions[] | select(.contractName==\"TipJar\") | .contractAddress' \\\n  packages/foundry/broadcast/Deploy.s.sol/31337/run-latest.json)\nexport ALICE_PK=0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80  # Anvil #0\n\ncast send $USDC \"approve(address,uint256)\" $JAR 25000000 --private-key $ALICE_PK --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $JAR  \"tip(uint256,string)\" 25000000 \"gm from the demo\" --private-key $ALICE_PK --rpc-url $RPC\n\n# Verify: jar balance, tip count, and the feed\ncast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" $JAR --rpc-url $RPC\ncast call $JAR  \"tipCount()(uint256)\" --rpc-url $RPC\ncast call $JAR  \"getTips()((address,uint256,uint256,string)[])\" --rpc-url $RPC\n```\n\n### Run the contract tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test\n```\n\nUnit tests (`packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol`) use a mock USDC and cover tipping,\napproval requirements, the feed, and owner-only withdrawal.\n\n---\n\n# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":714,"cache_read_input_tokens":77858,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":714},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"466c6798-159d-4c53-9ed9-93aaec68177f","uuid":"16e6e9b9-331f-413b-accf-59e6dbb9442d","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:09:39.000Z","request_id":"req_011CeCdhymcC1AzcXktR6Ucx"}

Demo","+tips move real USDC between local test identities by borrowing tokens from an existing","+Base holder (\"whale\") that we impersonate on the fork."," ","-## Requirements","+### Requirements"," ","-Before you begin, you need to install the following tools:","+- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/) — this repo was validated on Node 22","+- Yarn (v3+, bundled via `.yarn/releases`)","+- [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`anvil`, `cast`, `forge`)"," ","-- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)","-- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))","-- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)","+### 1. Install dependencies"," ","-## Quickstart","+```bash","+yarn install","+```"," ","-To get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:","+### 2. Terminal 1 — fork Base locally"," ","-1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:","+```bash","+yarn fork base","+```"," ","+This runs `anvil --fork-url base --chain-id 31337`, forking Base mainnet onto a local chain","+(chain id **31337**). The frontend targets `chains.foundry` (31337) in","+`packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`, so it points at this local fork, **not** Base itself.","+","+> **Live-timestamp demos:** Anvil only mines when a transaction arrives, so between tips","+> `block.timestamp` is frozen and the feed's \"time ago\" values would stall. For a running","+> demo, enable interval mining once the fork is up so time advances continuously:","+>","+> ```bash","+> cast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1","+> ```","+>","+> (Or start the fork with continuous mining from the beginning:","+> `yarn fork base --block-time 1`.)","+","+### 3. Terminal 2 — deploy the TipJar","+","+```bash","+yarn deploy"," ```","-cd my-dapp-example","-yarn install","-```"," ","-2. Run a local network in the first terminal:","+Deploys `TipJar` wired to Base USDC and regenerates the frontend ABIs in","+`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. On localhost this uses the built-in","+`scaffold-eth-default` account (Anvil account #9) as deployer/owner — no prompt."," ","+### 4. Terminal 3 — start the frontend","+","+```bash","+yarn start"," ```","-yarn chain","-```"," ","-This command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.","+Open http://localhost:3000. Connect a wallet on the **Foundry (31337)** network. The","+burner wallet works out of the box for local networks."," ","-3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:","+### 5. Fund a test identity with real USDC (whale impersonation)"," ","+The fork lets us hand real USDC to any local account by impersonating an existing Base","+holder. Below, `WHALE` is a Base address that already holds USDC on the fork, and `ALICE`","+is a local test identity (here, Anvil account #0) that will send the demo tip.","+","+```bash","+export RPC=http://127.0.0.1:8545","+export USDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913","+# A Base account holding USDC on the fork. Any current USDC holder works; verify its","+# balance first: cast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" <addr> --rpc-url $RPC","+export WHALE=0xcDAC0d6c6C59727a65F871236188350531885C43","+export ALICE=0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266   # Anvil account #0 (a test identity)","+","+# Let Anvil sign as the whale, and give it ETH for gas","+cast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC","+cast rpc anvil_setBalance $WHALE 0xde0b6b3a7640000 --rpc-url $RPC   # 1 ETH","+","+# Move 500 USDC (6 decimals) from the whale to Alice — real USDC, local fork only","+cast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" $ALICE 500000000 --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC","+","+# Confirm Alice received it","+cast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" $ALICE --rpc-url $RPC"," ```","-yarn deploy","-```"," ","-This command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.","+> If no suitable holder exists for a token, use `anvil_setBalance` / `anvil_setStorageAt`","+> to write balances directly. For USDC on Base, impersonating a real holder as above is","+> simplest and keeps the demo tips backed by genuine token transfers."," ","-4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:","+### 6. Send a tip"," ","+In the UI, connect as a test identity that holds USDC (import Alice's key into your wallet,","+or send USDC to your burner address in step 5). Tipping is two steps because `TipJar` pulls","+USDC via `transferFrom`:","+","+1. **Approve USDC** — approves the jar to spend the entered amount.","+2. **Send tip** — records the tip and moves the USDC into the jar. It appears in the feed.","+","+To demo the full loop from the CLI instead of the UI (Alice approves + tips 25 USDC):","+","+```bash","+export JAR=$(jq -r '.transactions[] | select(.contractName==\"TipJar\") | .contractAddress' \\","+  packages/foundry/broadcast/Deploy.s.sol/31337/run-latest.json)","+export ALICE_PK=0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80  # Anvil #0","+","+cast send $USDC \"approve(address,uint256)\" $JAR 25000000 --private-key $ALICE_PK --rpc-url $RPC","+cast send $JAR  \"tip(uint256,string)\" 25000000 \"gm from the demo\" --private-key $ALICE_PK --rpc-url $RPC","+","+# Verify: jar balance, tip count, and the feed","+cast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" $JAR --rpc-url $RPC","+cast call $JAR  \"tipCount()(uint256)\" --rpc-url $RPC","+cast call $JAR  \"getTips()((address,uint256,uint256,string)[])\" --rpc-url $RPC"," ```","-yarn start","+","+### Run the contract tests","+","+```bash","+yarn foundry:test"," ```"," ","-Visit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.","+Unit tests (`packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol`) use a mock USDC and cover tipping,","+approval requirements, the feed, and owner-only withdrawal."," ","-Run smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`","+---"," ","-- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`","-- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.","-- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`","+# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2"," ","+<h4 align=\"center\">","+  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |","+  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>","+</h4>"," ","+🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.","+","+> [!NOTE]","+> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.","+","+⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.","+","+- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.","+- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.","+- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.","+- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.","+- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.","+"," ## Documentation"," "," Visit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2."]},{"oldStart":80,"oldLines":4,"newStart":169,"newLines":4,"lines":[" "," We welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!"," ","-Please see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","\\ No newline at end of file","+Please see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2."]}],"originalFile":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","userModified":false}}
