{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCdmXWQayj4CNJ9WAhWJ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01BjPqqB2Z6GtsLt39fQjtDc","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T185850Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/DEPLOY.md","content":"# Deploying the USDC Tip Jar frontend to IPFS\n\nThe site ships as a **static export** hosted on IPFS. This file documents the exact build,\nupload, and post-deploy verification commands. **It does not upload anything** — run the\nupload step yourself when you're ready.\n\nThere are two independent things you can deploy:\n\n1. The **contract** to Base mainnet (only when you want a real, non-fork jar).\n2. The **static frontend** to IPFS (the primary deliverable here).\n\n---\n\n## 1. (Optional) Deploy the contract to Base\n\nThe frontend targets the local fork by default. To point it at a real Base deployment,\ndeploy the contract and add Base to the frontend's target networks.\n\n```bash\n# Generate/import a funded deployer keystore (never the scaffold-eth-default account)\nyarn generate            # or: yarn account:import\n\n# Deploy TipJar to Base mainnet\nyarn deploy --network base\n```\n\nThen, in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`, set `targetNetworks: [chains.base]` and\nrebuild. USDC is already the canonical Base token baked into the deploy script.\n\n> For a fork-only demo you can skip this section entirely — the frontend runs against the\n> local Anvil fork per [`README.md`](./README.md).\n\n---\n\n## 2. Build the static IPFS bundle\n\nThe IPFS build is gated behind `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` in\n`packages/nextjs/next.config.ts`, which sets `output: \"export\"`, `trailingSlash: true`, and\n`images.unoptimized: true` so every route is emitted as its own static directory.\n\nSet the **production origin** before building so Open Graph URLs/images resolve to the real\nhost instead of `localhost`. This scaffold reads `VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL` (a\nhostname, no scheme) in `utils/scaffold-eth/getMetadata.ts`. Use your ENS/gateway host.\n\nAlways remove old artifacts first so you never ship a stale `out/`:\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out\n\nVERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL=\"tipjar.example.eth.limo\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  yarn build\n```\n\n> **Node 25 note:** on Node 25 the built-in `localStorage` global can exist without the\n> standard Web Storage methods, which crashes libraries during static prerender. If you hit\n> that, prefix the build so the flag is inherited by build workers (not needed on Node 22,\n> which this repo was validated on):\n>\n> ```bash\n> NODE_OPTIONS=\"--no-experimental-webstorage\" \\\n>   VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL=\"tipjar.example.eth.limo\" \\\n>   NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n>   yarn build\n> ```\n\n### Pre-upload verification (in `packages/nextjs`)\n\nConfirm the intended content shipped, every route emitted its own directory, and metadata\ncarries the production origin — **not** localhost:\n\n```bash\n# One index.html per route (home, debug, blockexplorer, 404, ...)\nls out/index.html out/*/index.html\n\n# The home page actually contains the Tip Jar UI\ngrep -q \"USDC Tip Jar\" out/index.html && echo \"OK: tip jar page present\"\n\n# Open Graph origin is the production host, with no localhost leak\ngrep -o \"https://tipjar.example.eth.limo/thumbnail.jpg\" out/index.html\ntest \"$(grep -c 'localhost:3000' out/index.html)\" -eq 0 && echo \"OK: no localhost leak\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Upload to IPFS\n\n`out/` is the directory to pin. Use whichever pinning path you have credentials for — pick\n**one**:\n\n```bash\n# a) Local IPFS node / Kubo\nipfs add -r --cid-version 1 out\n# -> note the root CID printed on the last line\n\n# b) Pinning service CLI (example: web3.storage / w3)\nw3 up out\n```\n\nRecord the **root CID** it prints — you need it for verification and for the ENS content hash.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Post-deploy verification\n\nReplace `<cid>` with the CID from step 3 and `<gateway>` with your gateway host\n(e.g. `ipfs.io`, `dweb.link`, or your own).\n\n```bash\nexport CID=<cid>\nexport GATEWAY=<gateway>\n\n# 4a. Root loads\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"root: %{http_code}\\n\" -L \"https://$GATEWAY/ipfs/$CID/\"\n\n# 4b. A NON-home route resolves too (root success alone does NOT prove this).\n#     trailingSlash export means routes live at /<route>/ :\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"debug: %{http_code}\\n\" -L \"https://$GATEWAY/ipfs/$CID/debug/\"\n\n# 4c. Open Graph image + URL use the production origin (not localhost)\ncurl -s -L \"https://$GATEWAY/ipfs/$CID/\" | grep -oE 'og:(url|image)[^>]*' | head\n```\n\nExpect `200` (not `404`) for both 4a and 4b, and the production host in 4c.\n\n> **Unchanged CID?** If re-uploading yields the same CID, the bytes are byte-for-byte\n> identical — your change did not make it into `out/`. Re-check the build output and that\n> you rebuilt after removing `.next`/`out`, before blaming gateway caching.\n\n---\n\n## 5. (Optional) Point ENS at the release\n\nOnly after the deployed CID has been reviewed and approved:\n\n1. Set the ENS name's **content hash** to `ipfs://<cid>`.\n2. Confirm the resolver returns that CID and that a public gateway\n   (e.g. `https://<name>.limo`) serves it.\n\nDo not consider the release complete until steps 4a–4c pass and (if used) the ENS content\nhash resolves to the reviewed CID.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3711,"cache_read_input_tokens":78572,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3711},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"466c6798-159d-4c53-9ed9-93aaec68177f","uuid":"5e55551e-56da-492c-a55e-57e5ff2d68a8","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:10:09.734Z","request_id":"req_011CeCdmWJV4vVnP9SXmm8dq"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BjPqqB2Z6GtsLt39fQjtDc","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T185850Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/DEPLOY.md (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"466c6798-159d-4c53-9ed9-93aaec68177f","uuid":"e9357258-e5cb-4ca5-a357-b740f7e576b5","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:10:09.741Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-08-19T185850Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/DEPLOY.md","content":"# Deploying the USDC Tip Jar frontend to IPFS\n\nThe site ships as a **static export** hosted on IPFS. This file documents the exact build,\nupload, and post-deploy verification commands. **It does not upload anything** — run the\nupload step yourself when you're ready.\n\nThere are two independent things you can deploy:\n\n1. The **contract** to Base mainnet (only when you want a real, non-fork jar).\n2. The **static frontend** to IPFS (the primary deliverable here).\n\n---\n\n## 1. (Optional) Deploy the contract to Base\n\nThe frontend targets the local fork by default. To point it at a real Base deployment,\ndeploy the contract and add Base to the frontend's target networks.\n\n```bash\n# Generate/import a funded deployer keystore (never the scaffold-eth-default account)\nyarn generate            # or: yarn account:import\n\n# Deploy TipJar to Base mainnet\nyarn deploy --network base\n```\n\nThen, in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`, set `targetNetworks: [chains.base]` and\nrebuild. USDC is already the canonical Base token baked into the deploy script.\n\n> For a fork-only demo you can skip this section entirely — the frontend runs against the\n> local Anvil fork per [`README.md`](./README.md).\n\n---\n\n## 2. Build the static IPFS bundle\n\nThe IPFS build is gated behind `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` in\n`packages/nextjs/next.config.ts`, which sets `output: \"export\"`, `trailingSlash: true`, and\n`images.unoptimized: true` so every route is emitted as its own static directory.\n\nSet the **production origin** before building so Open Graph URLs/images resolve to the real\nhost instead of `localhost`. This scaffold reads `VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL` (a\nhostname, no scheme) in `utils/scaffold-eth/getMetadata.ts`. Use your ENS/gateway host.\n\nAlways remove old artifacts first so you never ship a stale `out/`:\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out\n\nVERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL=\"tipjar.example.eth.limo\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  yarn build\n```\n\n> **Node 25 note:** on Node 25 the built-in `localStorage` global can exist without the\n> standard Web Storage methods, which crashes libraries during static prerender. If you hit\n> that, prefix the build so the flag is inherited by build workers (not needed on Node 22,\n> which this repo was validated on):\n>\n> ```bash\n> NODE_OPTIONS=\"--no-experimental-webstorage\" \\\n>   VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL=\"tipjar.example.eth.limo\" \\\n>   NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n>   yarn build\n> ```\n\n### Pre-upload verification (in `packages/nextjs`)\n\nConfirm the intended content shipped, every route emitted its own directory, and metadata\ncarries the production origin — **not** localhost:\n\n```bash\n# One index.html per route (home, debug, blockexplorer, 404, ...)\nls out/index.html out/*/index.html\n\n# The home page actually contains the Tip Jar UI\ngrep -q \"USDC Tip Jar\" out/index.html && echo \"OK: tip jar page present\"\n\n# Open Graph origin is the production host, with no localhost leak\ngrep -o \"https://tipjar.example.eth.limo/thumbnail.jpg\" out/index.html\ntest \"$(grep -c 'localhost:3000' out/index.html)\" -eq 0 && echo \"OK: no localhost leak\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Upload to IPFS\n\n`out/` is the directory to pin. Use whichever pinning path you have credentials for — pick\n**one**:\n\n```bash\n# a) Local IPFS node / Kubo\nipfs add -r --cid-version 1 out\n# -> note the root CID printed on the last line\n\n# b) Pinning service CLI (example: web3.storage / w3)\nw3 up out\n```\n\nRecord the **root CID** it prints — you need it for verification and for the ENS content hash.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Post-deploy verification\n\nReplace `<cid>` with the CID from step 3 and `<gateway>` with your gateway host\n(e.g. `ipfs.io`, `dweb.link`, or your own).\n\n```bash\nexport CID=<cid>\nexport GATEWAY=<gateway>\n\n# 4a. Root loads\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"root: %{http_code}\\n\" -L \"https://$GATEWAY/ipfs/$CID/\"\n\n# 4b. A NON-home route resolves too (root success alone does NOT prove this).\n#     trailingSlash export means routes live at /<route>/ :\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"debug: %{http_code}\\n\" -L \"https://$GATEWAY/ipfs/$CID/debug/\"\n\n# 4c. Open Graph image + URL use the production origin (not localhost)\ncurl -s -L \"https://$GATEWAY/ipfs/$CID/\" | grep -oE 'og:(url|image)[^>]*' | head\n```\n\nExpect `200` (not `404`) for both 4a and 4b, and the production host in 4c.\n\n> **Unchanged CID?** If re-uploading yields the same CID, the bytes are byte-for-byte\n> identical — your change did not make it into `out/`. Re-check the build output and that\n> you rebuilt after removing `.next`/`out`, before blaming gateway caching.\n\n---\n\n## 5. (Optional) Point ENS at the release\n\nOnly after the deployed CID has been reviewed and approved:\n\n1. Set the ENS name's **content hash** to `ipfs://<cid>`.\n2. Confirm the resolver returns that CID and that a public gateway\n   (e.g. `https://<name>.limo`) serves it.\n\nDo not consider the release complete until steps 4a–4c pass and (if used) the ENS content\nhash resolves to the reviewed CID.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCdr9FqsB2Xv4xnTPNmw","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Everything is validated and the environment is cleaned up. I won't commit since you didn't ask. Here's what I built.\n\n## USDC Tip Jar for Base — done\n\n**Contract** (`packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`)\n- Accepts USDC tips via `transferFrom` using OpenZeppelin `SafeERC20`, `Ownable`, and `ReentrancyGuard` (v5.6.1, library-first per the OZ skill).\n- Records every tip on-chain (`tipper`, `amount`, `timestamp`, `message`) in a `tips[]` array with `getTips()`/`tipCount()` for the feed, plus `totalTipped` and per-address totals.\n- Owner-only `withdraw()`. Deploy script (`DeployTipJar.s.sol`) wires it to canonical Base USDC `0x8335…2913`.\n- Replaced the `YourContract` scaffolding; **9 Foundry tests pass** (tipping, approval requirement, feed order, events, owner-only withdraw).\n\n**Frontend** (`packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` + `_components/TipForm.tsx`, `TipFeed.tsx`)\n- Home page is the tip jar: live feed (newest-first, ENS `Address`, USDC amounts at 6 decimals) and an **approve-then-tip** form that reads balance/allowance and switches between \"Approve USDC\" and \"Send tip\".\n- USDC registered in `externalContracts.ts` under chain 31337 so the form talks to real forked USDC. Type-check passes, lint clean on my files.\n\n**Validated the whole loop against real Base state** on a local `anvil --fork-url base` (chain 31337): impersonated a real USDC whale → funded a test identity with 500 USDC → approved + tipped 25 USDC → confirmed the jar balance, `tipCount`, and `getTips` feed entry. No real money at risk, nothing broadcast.\n\n**Docs**\n- `README.md` — exact local workflow: `yarn fork base`, `yarn deploy`, `yarn start`, whale-impersonation funding commands, interval-mining note for live timestamps, and a CLI tip demo.\n- `DEPLOY.md` — the static IPFS build (verified: `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` export emits per-route `out/*/index.html`, OG origin set via `VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL` with **no localhost leak**), upload options, and post-deploy verification (root + non-home `/debug/` route return 200, OG origin check, unchanged-CID caveat, ENS content-hash step). **Nothing was uploaded**, as required.\n\nTwo notes worth flagging:\n- The playbook's env var `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL` doesn't drive metadata in this scaffold version — it reads `VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL` (hostname only). I verified that and documented the working var in DEPLOY.md.\n- The whale address in the README is one I verified on today's fork; it holds ~3.95M USDC, but I noted to re-check any holder's balance before relying on it.\n\nTo demo: `yarn install` → `yarn fork base` → `yarn deploy` → `yarn start`, then follow README step 5 to fund a test wallet."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1356,"cache_read_input_tokens":86928,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1356},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"466c6798-159d-4c53-9ed9-93aaec68177f","uuid":"0cfe9626-6278-4098-83fd-c399e1875442","timestamp":"2026-08-19T19:11:07.749Z","request_id":"req_011CeCdr8888q1pwvKuPHnKo"}

No real money at risk, nothing broadcast.\n\n**Docs**\n- `README.md` — exact local workflow: `yarn fork base`, `yarn deploy`, `yarn start`, whale-impersonation funding commands, interval-mining note for live timestamps, and a CLI tip demo.\n- `DEPLOY.md` — the static IPFS build (verified: `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` export emits per-route `out/*/index.html`, OG origin set via `VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL` with **no localhost leak**), upload options, and post-deploy verification (root + non-home `/debug/` route return 200, OG origin check, unchanged-CID caveat, ENS content-hash step). **Nothing was uploaded**, as required.\n\nTwo notes worth flagging:\n- The playbook's env var `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL` doesn't drive metadata in this scaffold version — it reads `VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL` (hostname only). I verified that and documented the working var in DEPLOY.md.\n- The whale address in the README is one I verified on today's fork; it holds ~3.95M USDC, but I noted to re-check any holder's balance before relying on it.\n\nTo demo: `yarn install` → `yarn fork base` → `yarn deploy` → `yarn start`, then follow README step 5 to fund a test wallet.","ttft_ms":2677,"ttft_stream_ms":1258,"time_to_request_ms":28,"type":"result","duration_ms":735861,"uuid":"d558ee19-b860-4be1-b13a-78bee5e3f637"}
